                                              fi
                                              if [ `command -v rpm` ]; then

                                              fi

                                              # failed to start due to denials from SELinux — useful for CentOS
                                              # and Fedora machines.  For Ubuntu (which runs AppArmor), DevStack
                                              # already captures the contents of /var/log/kern.log (via
                                              # `journalctl -t kernel` redirected into syslog.txt.gz), which
                                              # contains AppArmor-related messages.
                                              if [ -f /var/log/audit/audit.log ] ; then
                                                  sudo cp /var/log/audit/audit.log /home/zuul/audit.log &&
                                                  chmod +r /home/zuul/audit.log;
                                              fi

                                                  sudo gzip -r /var/core
                                                  sudo cp -r /var/core /home/zuul/
                                              fi

Apache logs are under apache/ thans to the\n# apache-logs-conf role.\ngrep -i deprecat /home/zuul/logs/*.txt /home/zuul/apache/*.log | \\\n    sed -r 's/[0-9]{1,2}\\:[0-9]{1,2}\\:[0-9]{1,2}\\.[0-9]{1,3}/ /g' | \\\n    sed -r 's/[0-9]{1,2}\\:[0-9]{1,2}\\:[0-9]{1,2}/ /g' | \\\n    sed -r 's/[0-9]{1,4}-[0-9]{1,2}-[0-9]{1,4}/ /g' |\n    sed -r 's/\\[.*\\]/ /g' | \\\n    sed -r 's/\\s[0-9]+\\s/ /g' | \\\n    awk '{if (\$0 in seen) {seen[\$0]++} else {out[++n]=\$0;seen[\$0]=1}} END { for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print seen[out[i]]\" :: \" out[i] }' > /home/zuul/deprecations.log\n"
